A hit is not a verdict. Association is by proximity, and proximity is a heuristic: a page
may legitimately say it does not cover something another page does, and the token nearest
a claim may not be its subject. Each pair is a candidate for a human to read - the value is
that nobody can eyeball every such claim across the corpus.
